fills_per_year_28 * patients_per_medication\n        annual_drug_cost_56 = (price_per_1000 / Decimal(\"1000\")) * capsules_per_fill_56 * fills_per_year_56 * patients_per_medication\n\n        # Packaging costs:\n        # - 28-day model uses the card cost matched by the medication's blister_card_count.\n        # - 56-day model prefers a row for doubled blister_card_count if present; otherwise\n        #   assumes per-fill cost scales linearly with the number of blister cards.\n        blister_card_count_56 = blister_card_count * 2\n        if blister_card_count_56 in card_cost_by_count:\n            card_cost_usd_56 = d(card_cost_by_count[blister_card_count_56][\"card_cost_usd\"])\n        else:\n            card_cost_usd_56 = card_cost_usd * Decimal(\"2\")\n\n        annual_packaging_cost_28 = card_cost_usd * patients_per_medication * fills_per_year_28\n        annual_packaging_cost_56 = card_cost_usd_56 * patients_per_medication * fills_per_year_56\n\n        annual_reimbursement_28 = reimbursement_per_cycle_180 * fills_per_year_28\n        annual_reimbursement_56 = reimbursement_per_cycle_180 * fills_per_year_56\n\n        annual_margin_28 = annual_reimbursement_28 - annual_drug_cost_28 - annual_packaging_cost_28\n        annual_margin_56 = annual_reimbursement_56 - annual_drug_cost_56 - annual_packaging_cost_56\n\n        annual_margin_diff = annual_margin_56 - annual_margin_28\n\n        med_obj = {\n            \"medication\": med,\n            \"price_per_1000_capsules_usd\": float(q2(price_per_1000)),\n            \"blister_card_count\": blister_card_count,\n            \"card_cost_usd\": float(q2(card_cost_usd)),\n            \"reimbursement_per_cycle_180_patients_usd\": float(q2(reimbursement_per_cycle_180)),\n            \"annual_drug_cost_28_day_usd\": float(q2(annual_drug_cost_28)),\n            \"annual_drug_cost_56_day_usd\": float(q2(annual_drug_cost_56)),\n            \"annual_packaging_cost_28_day_usd\": float(q2(annual_packaging_cost_28)),\n            \"annual_packaging_cost_56_day_usd\": float(q2(annual_packaging_cost_56)),\n            \"annual_reimbursement_28_day_usd\": float(q2(annual_reimbursement_28)),\n            \"annual_reimbursement_56_day_usd\": float(q2(annual_reimbursement_56)),\n            \"annual_margin_28_day_usd\": float(q2(annual_margin_28)),\n            \"annual_margin_56_day_usd\": float(q2(annual_margin_56)),\n            \"annual_margin_difference_56_minus_28_usd\": float(q2(annual_margin_diff)),\n        }\n\n        medications.append(med_obj)\n\n    total_margin_28 = q2(sum(Decimal(str(m[\"annual_margin_28_day_usd\"])) for m in medications))\n    total_margin_56 = q2(sum(Decimal(str(m[\"annual_margin_56_day_usd\"])) for m in medications))\n    total_margin_diff = q2(sum(Decimal(str(m[\"annual_margin_difference_56_minus_28_usd\"])) for m in medications))\n    absolute_total_diff = q2(abs(total_margin_diff))\n\n    decision = \"convert_to_56_day\" if absolute_total_diff < switch_threshold_usd else \"keep_28_day\"\n\n    analysis = {\n        \"assumptions\": {\n            \"patients_per_medication\": int(patients_per_medication),\n            \"fills_per_year_28_day\": int(fills_per_year_28),\n            \"fills_per_year_56_day\": int(fills_per_year_56),\n            \"capsules_per_fill_28_day\": int(capsules_per_fill_28),\n            \"capsules_per_fill_56_day\": int(capsules_per_fill_56),\n            \"switch_threshold_usd\": float(q2(switch_threshold_usd)),\n        },\n        \"medications\": medications,\n        \"totals\": {\n            \"total_annual_margin_28_day_usd\": float(total_margin_28),\n            \"total_annual_margin_56_day_usd\": float(total_margin_56),\n            \"total_annual_margin_difference_56_minus_28_usd\": float(total_margin_diff),\n            \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": float(absolute_total_diff),\n        },\n        \"recommendation\": {\n            \"decision\": decision,\n            \"justification\": (\n                f\"Absolute total annual margin difference is ${float(absolute_total_diff):.2f}, \"\n                f\"threshold is ${float(q2(switch_threshold_usd)):.2f}.\"\n            ),\n        },\n    }\n\n    with open(OUT_JSON, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(analysis, f, indent=2)\n        f.write(\"\\n\")\n\n    with open(OUT_MD, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(f\"Total annual margin (28-day):
